Critical Discography

Catalogue Entries Gallery Review

Human review board

Queued identity matches for the seed. Needs-review rows show seed vs harvest side by side; empty fields stay blank. Community notes help authoring; they stay on a separate layer from the owner’s editorial voice. Citation tasks are listed in the tally only — not on identity rows.

103accept-eligible
200needs review
89wrong work
510citation tasks
359unharvested
Owner only (NZA-93)
There are no public accept / reject / defer buttons on this page — community cannot apply MBIDs. The owner records decisions in proposals/review-decisions.json (fill by + date on each accept), then runs make review-apply or the Review apply workflow (repository owner actor required). Identity apply writes the release-group MBID only. Agents never write scores or data/statements/.
Community layer
Anyone signed into GitHub can open a community review comment. Comments render here with an amber label and are stored under data/community/ — never merged into data/editorial/ or assessment statements.
Honest omit
Fassung, completeness, session year and live/studio appear on a row only when MusicBrainz actually supplied a token. They are not repeated as empty “absent Fassung” paragraphs. seed.year and MB first-release stay labelled as release-year proxies, never as session year. Match figures are MusicBrainz search confidence, not critical verdicts and never stars. Inventory: PAYLOAD_GAPS.md.
